# The INCBIN census

Generated by `tools/research/incbin_census.py` (`python tools/research/incbin_census.py`);
the text is a pure function of the retail image, the source tree and that script's region
table, so two runs are byte-identical.

ROM `baserom.gba` sha256 `940ad5f01db4465b8877dfe739510cbf34f4ea3d390f3df13519808bc36f059e`.

## SC1 - the bytes

| item | value |
|---|---|
| gate INCBIN total (`gate.incbin_ranges()`) | **494250 B** |
| INCBIN slices | 3128 |
| census rows | 3128 |
| census bytes | **494250 B** |
| equality | EXACT |

Status is one of two words:

- `raw-labeled` - a tracked R-RAW-001 fragment carries this exact range AND its own comment
  for that symbol names a consumer. The bytes are extracted retail data a consumer cites;
  no structure, layout or extent is claimed.
- `unreferenced` - no consumer names this range by any route the project has measured (code
  xrefs, pointer fields, format scans, the loader trace, the runtime session). The row
  carries a `unk_<ROMADDR>` symbol and this status, and nothing else is claimed about it.

### By status

| status | bytes | share | rows |
|---|---|---|---|
| `raw-labeled` | 393069 | 79.53 % | 643 |
| `unreferenced` | 101181 | 20.47 % | 2485 |
| **total** | **494250** | **100.00 %** | **3128** |

## The classes (PATH-100-015, and the gate's citation measurement)

Every byte of the image is source or raw-data. The census splits the raw-data bytes by its own
two statuses above; the gate prints a FOUR-way split of the same bytes, measured by
`tools/research/citations_in_source.py` (`docs/research/RAW_CITATIONS.csv` carries the
per-row evidence) and QUOTED here from `gate.raw_classes()` rather than re-derived:

| class | bytes | share |
|---|---|---|
| source - built from tracked source or a byte-exact generator | 7894358 | 94.1081 % |
| raw-data, `raw-labeled` - the census's own status, a fragment's comment names a consumer | 393069 | 4.6857 % |
| raw-data, `unreferenced` - the census's own status, no comment names a consumer | 101181 | 1.2062 % |
| **accounted** | **8388608** | **100.0000 %** |

The gate's four classes of those same raw-data bytes, quoted from `gate.raw_classes()`:

| class | bytes | share of the raw-data total |
|---|---|---|
| `cited` - a citation site derived from `code_xref` lies in the ledger's code map (P or A) | 291487 | 58.9756 % |
| `runtime-cited` - not cited, and the carrier comment says `cited by runtime read` | 28800 | 5.8270 % |
| `unreferenced` - no code site and no runtime read | 173963 | 35.1974 % |
| **raw-data** | **494250** | **100.0000 %** |

`cited` is an UPPER bound: a site in a ledger code row is still a sweep site, so until
instruction boundaries are proven a row called `cited` is one a linear sweep reaches, not one
a running program was observed to read.

## The raw-labeled runs (R-RAW-001)

A region with a cited code consumer but no code-derived extent is carried as LABELED RAW
DATA: `tools/research/rawlabeled.py` writes a fragment per run - one `unk_<ROMADDR>` symbol
per cited base, with its loader citation, stride and shape - and every symbol's bytes are ONE
`INCBIN` of a build-time extracted slice of `baserom.gba` (PATH-100-015), so no retail byte
payload is tracked and `gate.incbin_ranges()` counts every one of those bytes as `raw-data`.
`asm/rest_of_the_game.s` INCLUDEs each fragment where the retail INCBIN slice was. Carried
whole means no layout is claimed; the citation channel is `code_xref.py`'s pc-relative VALUE
channel, one level past the pool word a site computes (P-422), or an explicit citation list
(`--citations`: a runtime read, `pc` plus the exact address, one symbol per row). The table
below is PATH-100-001's own two runs, whose tool is now folded into the same module.

| run | bytes | symbols | loaders cited |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x74D248..0x74F058` | 7696 | 22 | 77 |
| `0x7B5B64..0x7B79A4` | 7744 | 26 | 91 |
| **total** | **15440** | **48** | **168** |

## RECON-LATE-001's 110,733 B, accounted for

The union basis is the SPEC's disjoint byte mask; the sum basis is V1's round trip. The
families merge to the 110,733 B the ticket names, and every family now has a promotion or a
status:

| family | union B | sum B | promoted B | status | citation |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| AI behaviour node records (both slices) | 68140 | 68320 | 53152 | `promoted-since-recon` | SPEC-AINODE-001; 2,504 records / 53,152 B in-block, the 16 B stub in the code region stays retail |
| row table 0x70F514 | 6624 | 6624 | 6624 | `promoted-since-recon` | the dispatcher 0x0800AA9C; promoted in this ticket (INCBIN-CENSUS-001), which this census's own promoted-family table re-derives |
| enemy stat records 0x711274 | 4228 | 4228 | 4200 | `promoted-since-recon` | SPEC-ENEMYSTAT-001-R2; the empty 151st slot (28 B) stays retail |
| event-resource index table 0x79B704 | 2088 | 2088 | 2088 | `promoted-since-recon` | SPEC-INDEX-001-R2 |
| dialogue record words (slice A) | 29633 | 29632 | 0 | `identified-with-consumer` | K-017 consumer chain; NOT promoted and not promotable yet - the engine walk reaches 3,850 8-byte words but only 2,101 of 3,849 neighbour gaps are exactly 8 B and 52 B of the reached extents overlap, so the record length is not established (the ticket's request and handoff both say it was not promoted) |
| global-state pointer table 0x6FC238 | 20 | 0 | 40 | `promoted-since-recon` | SPEC-GSTATE-001-R2; the SPEC sized it 20 B, the promoting ticket measured 40 B (six IWRAM then four ROM words), which is the one 20 B correction to the union total |
| **total** | **110733** | **110892** | **66104** | | |

The 110,733 B union reproduces exactly (the global-state table is quoted at the SPEC's 20 B;
the promoting ticket measured 40 B). Promoted since the baseline: 66104 B. Not yet promoted:
44629 B union - the dialogue record words (consumer-backed, but not promoted: the record
length is not established) and the AI node records the row table does not reach (no consumer
names them).

## What this census does not do

- It promotes nothing itself. Promotion is the emitter's job, one family at a time, inside the
  end-of-ROM block; the raw-labeled ranges are carried by `tools/research/rawlabeled.py`, which
  parses nothing and claims no extent beyond the cited addresses.
- It says nothing about runtime behaviour: every status is static evidence.
